# Kiểm kê từng mục (§5.2) · bắt đầu 2026-09-18

Plan: `docs/plans/2026-09-18-item-inventory.md`. Kiểm kê mức registry, thứ tự ưu tiên D3 và ba lượt từng mục đã làm trước (frontend-design, code-review + pr-review-toolkit, claude-code-setup): `docs/specs/2026-09-11-skill-inventory.md` — các quyết định ở đó được trỏ về, không quyết lại.

**Cách đọc một dòng.** `Mục` là `` `<nhãn>:<đường dẫn trong nguồn>` ``; `Loại` và `Dòng` do `scripts/inventory-items.cjs list` in ra (skill: số dòng của `SKILL.md`); `Quyết định` là một trong ba giá trị của §5.2: **absorb** (lấy chữ vào `references/` của skill đích, có ghi nguồn — chỉ khi giấy phép cho phép), **idea** (lấy ý, viết lại, không lấy chữ), **drop** (kèm lý do). Một mục đúng một skill đích.

**Kiểm độ phủ** (mỗi nguồn): `node scripts/inventory-items.cjs check docs/specs/2026-09-18-item-inventory.md <thư mục nguồn> <nhãn>` phải báo đủ mọi mục. Thư mục nguồn là bản sao trong `_build/upstream/`, đúng sha ghi ở đầu mỗi phần.

**Ba dạng dòng đặc biệt** (thêm 2026-09-18, từ đợt 4). `group/<nhóm>`: một nhóm file của `awesome-cursorrules`; `Dòng` là số file, và danh sách file nằm ở mục "Thành viên nhóm" của nguồn đó. `(tool)`: chính công cụ của một nguồn chạy bên cạnh agent; không phải đường dẫn, nên `check` không đối. Skill đích `kit`: máy móc của chính kit (kênh cài đặt, định dạng test, benchmark), không phải một skill. Bản sao `.codex/` của Spartan nằm trong một bảng đối chiếu riêng, mỗi bản sao theo quyết định của dòng gốc. Bảng Tổng đếm theo mục: một dòng nhóm tính bằng số file của nhóm, một bản sao tính là một mục.

## obra/superpowers · đợt 1

`https://github.com/obra/superpowers` tag `v5.1.0`, commit `f2cbfbefebbf`, MIT. Bản sao: `_build/upstream/obra_superpowers` (clone 2026-09-18, sha khớp `upstream/sources.json`). 15 mục: 14 skill, 1 hooks.

Đây là "gap-check" mà D3 giao cho nguồn này (hạng 1): mọi mục đều đã có đích từ lượt port 2026-09-11 (`derived` trong `upstream/sources.json`). Không mục nào còn treo. Hai mục chỉ được lấy **một phần**; phần không lấy ghi trong lý do.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## mattpocock/skills · đợt 1

`https://github.com/mattpocock/skills` commit `3cca18b368ae`, MIT, mode `adapt`. Bản sao: `_build/upstream/mattpocock_skills` (sha khớp `upstream/sources.json`, kiểm 2026-09-18). 37 mục, đều là skill.

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập (đọc cả 37 `SKILL.md` và mọi file đi kèm), phiên chính đọc lại từng dòng và giữ nguyên các quyết định: 10 absorb, 11 idea, 16 drop. Ba điểm để lại cho sprint của skill đích, không sửa ở đây: (1) `grilling` hỏi cả loạt câu mỗi vòng, trái với "mỗi tin một câu" của `bk-spec/references/brainstorming.md` — phải chọn một khi viết; (2) `diagnosing-bugs` xếp hạng nhiều giả thuyết trước khi thử, còn `systematic-debugging.md` viết từng giả thuyết một — đọc là "xếp nhiều, thử từng cái" thì hợp, nhưng chữ phải khớp; (3) `tdd` muốn đưa refactor ra khỏi vòng lặp — không lấy, kit giữ refactor là bước 5. Tín hiệu cho catalog: `research` → `bk-research` (idea), `wizard` → `bk-ops` (idea), `loop-me` → `bk-agent` (idea), `git-guardrails-claude-code` → `bk-guard` (idea); phần nhánh hiệu năng của `diagnosing-bugs` và expand–contract của `to-tickets` là nguyên liệu cho `bk-perf` và `bk-db` khi hai skill đó được dựng.

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

#### (a) Work for bk-map, bk-research, bk-perf, bk-db, bk-ops or a pack

- **bk-research**: `research` (idea, row 10) — trace each claim to the primary source that owns it; findings saved as a cited file in the repository.
- **bk-perf**: `diagnosing-bugs` has a performance branch (a baseline measurement first, then bisect; logs rarely help). It rides with the bk-debug absorb until bk-perf exists.
- **bk-db**: the expand–contract sequence in `to-tickets` (add the new form beside the old, migrate readers in batches, then remove the old) is the standard safe schema change. bk-db and bk-build's migration step can point at the same reference.
- **bk-ops**: `wizard` (idea, row 18) — human-only provisioning, credentials and cutovers. Its template enters secrets hidden and prints only key names, so the idea is compatible with the kit's secret rule.
- **bk-map**: `improve-codebase-architecture` scopes exploration by the commit-history hot spots; the same move suits mapping an unfamiliar codebase.
- **bk-agent**: `loop-me` (idea, row 21) — trigger, checkpoint pushed as late as possible, decision-ready brief.
- **bk-guard**: `git-guardrails-claude-code` (idea, row 27). The brief names bk-guard without describing it; its scope is inferred from the name.
- **bk-preview**: the dropped HTML report of `improve-codebase-architecture` (Tailwind and Mermaid from CDNs, a before/after diagram per candidate) is the only visual-report material in this source.
- **bk-design / bk-ux**: the UI branch of `prototype` (three structurally different variants on the real route, switched by a URL parameter) is absorbed under bk-spec; bk-design can point to it.
- **bk-product**: `to-questionnaire` (idea, row 35) also serves stakeholder discovery.

#### (b) Conflicts with earlier decisions

- No item of this source was decided before, so no decision is changed.
- Registry hints (notes, not decisions), checked against the kit:
  - grill-me → bk-spec: grill-me is a wrapper; its substance, `grilling`, is absorbed into bk-spec.
  - diagnose → bk-debug: renamed upstream to `diagnosing-bugs` (CHANGELOG, commit `47bde84`); absorbed into bk-debug.
  - triage → bk-debug: not followed. bk-debug already reproduces; what triage adds (the already-implemented and previously-rejected checks) belongs to bk-spec.
  - zoom-out → bk-audit: no such item at `3cca18b`; the CHANGELOG records its removal as unused (commit `47bde84`). bk-audit gets its architecture lens from `improve-codebase-architecture` instead.
  - handoff → bk-close: same intent; decided as a drop because bk-close is stronger (committed, verified against git).
- Conflicts with text the kit already absorbed from obra/superpowers. Each needs the owner's choice when the absorb is written:
  1. `grilling` asks the whole question frontier per round; `bk-spec/references/brainstorming.md` says one question per message (while `bk-plan` already asks three to eight at once).
  2. `diagnosing-bugs` ranks three to five falsifiable hypotheses before testing any; `bk-debug/references/systematic-debugging.md` phase 3 writes one hypothesis at a time. Compatible if read as "rank several, test one at a time"; the wording needs reconciling.
  3. `tdd` moves refactoring out of the loop into review; `bk-test/references/tdd.md` keeps refactor as step 5. Not carried.
  4. `to-spec` and `triage`'s agent briefs avoid file paths and line numbers because they go stale; the kit anchors claims as `file:line`. Not carried.
  5. `implement-spec` passes context pointers to subagents; `bk-build/references/executing.md` pastes the full task text. Not carried.
  6. `ask-matt` and `handoff` treat a handoff as narrow and write it outside the repository; the kit commits one at the end of every session. Only the phase-boundary idea is carried.

#### (c) Could not read

- Nothing. All 37 `SKILL.md` files and every file beside them were read; the `agents/openai.yaml` files are Codex display metadata only. The line counts in the items file match the `SKILL.md` files at `3cca18b`.

## anthropics/claude-plugins-official · đợt 1

`https://github.com/anthropics/claude-plugins-official` commit `3b600518a637`, Apache-2.0 ở gốc repo, mode `adapt`. Bản sao: `_build/upstream/claude-plugins-official` (sha khớp `upstream/sources.json`). 121 mục: 30 command, 31 skill, 32 agent, 6 hooks, 22 plugin chỉ có cấu hình (MCP, LSP, output style).

**Giấy phép không đồng nhất trong repo — phát hiện của lượt này.** `plugins/claude-security/LICENSE` là **độc quyền**: giữ mọi quyền và cấm dùng bất kỳ phần nào để làm sản phẩm không phải của Anthropic hoặc cạnh tranh. Mười mục của plugin đó đều drop, và lấy cả ý của nó cũng cần owner quyết. Mười plugin trong `external_plugins/` không có file giấy phép riêng; tất cả đều drop nên không quyết định nào dựa vào chúng.

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập (đọc cả 121 mục); phiên chính đọc lại mọi dòng absorb và idea và các drop của những plugin dễ có giá trị (`claude-security`, `commit-commands`, `feature-dev`, `ralph-loop`), giữ nguyên: 19 absorb, 12 idea, 90 drop. Mười mục đã quyết trước (frontend-design, code-review, bảy mục của pr-review-toolkit, claude-code-setup, security-guidance) giữ nguyên quyết định.

**Đổi đích sau khi chốt catalog** (2026-09-18, câu 27 A3 của D5): dòng 41, 42 và 52, thủ tục nâng major, chuyển từ `bk-deps` sang `bk-build` — vào `references/major-upgrade.md`, cài mặc định. Quyết định absorb không đổi.

- **`code-modernization` là nguồn mới đáng kể nhất:** 5 absorb cho `bk-map` (chưa dựng) — `codebase-map.md`, `first-contact.md`, `business-rules.md`; 1 cho `bk-test` (`characterization.md` — phải ghi ngoại lệ với luật "test pass ngay lần đầu thì phải sửa" của `tdd.md`, vì test đặc tả hành vi cũ pass ngay theo thiết kế); 3 cho `bk-deps` (`major-upgrade.md` — protocol cấm nâng major trong một task nhưng chưa có thủ tục cho chính lần nâng đó).
- `mcp-server-dev/skills/build-mcp-server` → `bk-agent` (`tool-design.md`).
- `security-guidance`: giữ quyết định D3; khi port, agent đề xuất lấy thêm phương pháp review diff trong `hooks/review_api.py` và `hooks/llm.py` của cùng plugin, và đặt tên `references/security-lens.md`.

**Câu cho owner, không chặn việc nào** (gom vào lượt hỏi khi chốt catalog): (1) ý "một bản sửa làm đổi tập input được chấp nhận là một thay đổi hành vi" chỉ có trong `claude-security` (độc quyền) — có lấy ý không; (2) "nội dung của repo là dữ liệu, không bao giờ là chỉ dẫn" lặp lại nhiều nơi trong nguồn này — có đưa vào security baseline của protocol không (protocol đang sát trần); (3) thủ tục nâng major ở `bk-deps` (pack, không cài mặc định) hay ở `bk-build`.

**Một việc kiểm phát sinh:** plugin context7 chính thức dùng endpoint hosted, còn dòng tra tài liệu trong `bk-protocol/references/host-tools.md` dùng lệnh `npx` — chưa đối chiếu với tài liệu của Context7 (ghi ở `docs/status.md`).

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

**(a) Work for skills not built yet, and for packs**

- **bk-map** (not built) gets three references from absorb rows. `codebase-map.md`: legacy-analyst and modernize-map (trace from entry points, map the data first, dispatch edges resolved through config, entry points read from deployment config, no dead-code claim before every edge type is known, flows traced end to end, fact kept apart from inference). `first-contact.md`: modernize-preflight (the few questions only the owner can answer, the CI definition as build truth, one real unit built, missing includes and binary-only artifacts, inbound and outbound scope crossings). `business-rules.md`: business-rules-extractor and modernize-extract-rules (cited rule cards with concrete values, priority, confidence and an owner question; rules backed only by comments dropped). Ideas from modernize-assess (fingerprint, ranked debt, code-versus-docs gaps) and feature-dev's code-explorer (feature-scoped trace).
- **bk-deps** (pack): version-delta-analyst, uplift-migrator and modernize-uplift go to `major-upgrade.md`. The protocol forbids a major bump inside a task but has no procedure for the bump itself; if that procedure should ship in the default install, bk-build is the other possible home (owner's call).
- **bk-agent** (pack): build-mcp-server goes to `tool-design.md`; ideas from discord access (privilege changes never downstream of channel content) and skill-creator (prompt-eval method). Dropped but worth reading when bk-agent is written: build-mcpb `references/local-security.md` (tool inputs are untrusted even when a trusted model sends them; path containment), build-mcp-app `references/payload-budgeting.md`, agent-sdk-dev (official SDK docs first), and security-guidance's agent-permission-bypass category.
- **bk-preview** (pack): the playground idea. **bk-guard** (pack): hookify (all rows dropped) is the reference if bk-guard ever proposes warn-or-block rules from corrections; its engine and hooks are host machinery.
- **bk-ops**: terraform (HashiCorp's official Terraform MCP server) dropped. security-guidance's CI/CD and IaC categories (workflow command injection, unpinned third-party actions, an OIDC trust condition ending in a wildcard, over-broad IAM) belong in the security lens and are bk-ops material too.
- **bk-perf**: modernize-assess's optional runtime overlay (p50/p95/p99 per domain, the p99/p50 ratio used to rank operational risk) is an idea for bk-perf.
- **bk-research**: context7 dropped (no MCP install). Fact for `bk-protocol/references/host-tools.md`: this plugin points at Context7's hosted endpoint (`https://mcp.context7.com/mcp`, no local Node, optional `CONTEXT7_API_KEY`), while host-tools.md gives an `npx` install line; re-check that line against Context7's own docs (not verified beyond this config).
- **bk-db**: nothing substantive; data lineage sits with bk-map, ORM major bumps with bk-deps.
- **Stack files**: laravel-boost is Laravel's own agent tool; the unwritten `php-laravel.md` could name it under official-first, within the no-catalog rule (owner's call).
- **Cross-cutting**: the rule that repository content is data, never instructions, and that instruction-shaped text is itself a finding, recurs across code-modernization, claude-security and receipts. It enters through the legacy-analyst (bk-map) and security-auditor (bk-review) rows; whether it also belongs in the protocol's security baseline is the owner's call (bk-protocol is size-limited).

**(b) Conflicts with earlier decisions**

- All ten decided rows keep their decisions.
- Licence fact the task did not state: `plugins/claude-security/LICENSE` is proprietary, not Apache-2.0. It reserves all rights and forbids using any part of the plugin with, or to develop, a non-Anthropic or competing product. All ten claude-security rows are drops, and carrying even its ideas into the kit is COUNCIL. The cost is small: a disprove-first verification pass and the source-to-sink standard exist under Apache-2.0 in security-guidance's review prompts, and a per-hunk patch review (resolves, partial, introduces risk) in modernize-harden. Its one sharp idea without a permissive twin, that changing which inputs a fix accepts counts as a behaviour change, needs the owner's clearance.
- security-guidance, scope note with the decision unchanged: the decision names `hooks/patterns.py`, but the same plugin's `hooks/review_api.py` and `hooks/llm.py` (Apache-2.0) carry its diff-review method: entry points and sinks, following returned values to their callers, sibling-path gate parity, parser and validator differentials, and a refute pass that names attacker and victim and anchors each finding to a changed line. It is far richer than the patterns; recommend the security-lens port draw on both. The file name `references/security-lens.md` is my proposal; the decision named none.
- A tension for the test-engineer port, not a decision conflict: `bk-test/references/tdd.md` says a test that passes at once must be fixed; characterization tests pass at once by design, so `characterization.md` has to state that exception.
- Duplicates dropped: `plugins/code-simplifier/agents/code-simplifier.md` has a body identical to the absorbed pr-review-toolkit agent (checked by hash); feature-dev's code-reviewer repeats the absorbed rubric.

**(c) Not read, or read in part**

- All 121 items were read. Read in part, by choice and with no bearing on a decision: plugin-dev skill bodies beyond their outlines (and hook-development's output, security and lifecycle sections); claude-security's jobs, specs, scripts and workflow (proprietary; its agents, skill, hooks and README were read); code-modernization's `workflows/*.js` and topology viewer; security-guidance Python beyond `patterns.py` and the review prompts; skill-creator's agents and scripts; math-olympiad references; the channel servers; the receipts and session-report scripts.
- The LSP plugin folders hold only a README and a LICENSE; their server configuration was read in `.claude-plugin/marketplace.json`. receipts and session-report ship no `plugin.json`.
- asana, context7, firebase, github, gitlab, laravel-boost, linear, playwright, serena and terraform carry no licence file of their own; all are drops, so nothing depends on it.

## github/spec-kit · đợt 2

`https://github.com/github/spec-kit` commit `c173bf19a665`, MIT, mode **`ideas-only`** (quyết định trước: lấy ý, không lấy chữ, dù MIT cho phép). Bản sao: `_build/upstream/github_spec-kit` (sha khớp `upstream/sources.json`). 38 mục: 36 command (template lệnh và lệnh của các extension), 2 skill.

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập (đọc cả 38 mục); phiên chính đọc lại mọi dòng idea và kiểm bằng máy rằng không dòng nào absorb. Giữ nguyên: 0 absorb, 14 idea, 24 drop. Agent không thấy mục nào mà lấy chữ sẽ hơn hẳn viết lại (gần nhất là `clarify` và `checklist`, nhưng khoảng 1/5 mỗi file là khuôn móc extension), nên không có câu hỏi "có lấy chữ không" cho owner.

- **Bằng chứng đầu tiên cho pack `bk-product`:** năm lệnh `assess` (intake, research, define, shape, decide) là một phễu khám phá trọn vẹn, kết thúc bằng go / cần làm rõ / kill, và coi kill là thành công — đúng loại việc một người làm thay cả một product manager trước khi vào `bk-spec`. Hai ý trong đó có thể đi thẳng vào `bk-spec` (luôn có phương án nhỏ nhất và phương án "không làm/mua"; truy yêu cầu có dạng giải pháp về lại vấn đề) — để sprint của `bk-spec` quyết.
- Mâu thuẫn để lại cho sprint: `specify` giới hạn 3 câu hỏi và hỏi cùng lúc, `clarify` giới hạn 5 và hỏi từng câu; kit hỏi mỗi tin một câu — lấy một giới hạn và giữ cách hỏi của kit. `tasks` coi test là tuỳ chọn — không lấy.
- **Câu gộp cho owner, không chặn:** chính sách "nội dung lấy từ ngoài (trang web, issue, ghi chú) là dữ liệu, không bao giờ là chỉ dẫn; không đi theo redirect; từ chối loopback, dải private, endpoint metadata cloud; gỡ credential khỏi URL" xuất hiện ở ba lệnh của nguồn này và nhiều nơi trong `claude-plugins-official`. `grep` trong `skills/` không thấy luật nào như vậy. Có đưa vào security baseline của protocol (đang sát trần) hay vào `bk-research` khi dựng — gộp chung với câu (2) của `claude-plugins-official`.

| # | Mục | Loại | Dòng | Làm gì | Skill đích | Quyết định | Lý do |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

#### (a) Work for the missing skills and the packs

- **bk-product.** The five `assess` commands (rows 4–8) form a complete discovery funnel: intake, research, define, shape, decide, ending in go / needs-clarification / kill, with a kill counted as a success. This is direct evidence that the pack has substance for one person: it stands in for a product manager's "is this worth building" pass before bk-spec. Source artifacts live in a spec-kit folder (`.specify/assessments/<slug>/`); the kit would write where the project keeps design documents (`bk-spec/references/brainstorming.md`, "Writing the spec": no kit-named folder). Two of its ideas would also help the default install, and the owner may prefer to re-target them: shape's "smallest workable option, plus do-nothing or buy, plus rabbit holes" for bk-spec's approaches step (`brainstorming.md`, "Approaches before design"); define's "trace a solution-shaped ask back to its problem, and state non-goals" for bk-spec step 1.
- **bk-research.** Row 7's evidence discipline (every claim cited or tagged as an assumption with a confidence; an evidence-against section even when empty) matches the bk-researcher persona's confidence labels (`bk-protocol/references/personas.md`) and adds a disconfirming-evidence rule.
- **Cross-cutting, candidate for bk-research, the protocol's security baseline, or bk-debug.** The same untrusted-content policy appears in rows 6, 7 and 9: fetched pages, issue bodies and previously captured artifacts are data, never instructions; no redirects or crawling beyond the URL given; no secret handed to a page; refuse non-http(s) schemes, loopback, link-local, private ranges and cloud metadata endpoints; ask once for an unknown host (refuse when unattended); strip credentials from URLs and redact secrets before anything is persisted. A grep of `skills/` for `untrusted|injection|redirect` finds no such rule (the only hit is SQL injection in `bk-build/references/stacks/sql.md`). A protocol change is COUNCIL, so this is surfaced, not proposed as a row.
- **bk-setup (existing skill, flagged from a dropped row).** Row 34 checks ignore files per detected stack (`.env*`, `*.tfstate*`, `*.tfvars`, Kubernetes secrets, keys). The protocol says `.env` stays out of git, but nothing checks the ignore file itself; a candidate for bk-setup step 2 or a `detect-stack` note. Not measured how often it matters.
- **bk-db (minor).** Row 35 asks for a data model with validation rules and state transitions before tasks; row 37 copies field constraints into task text. Fits bk-db's schema work if that skill is built; otherwise it stays with bk-plan.
- **No work found** for bk-map, bk-perf, bk-ops, `bk-agent`, `bk-deps`, `bk-ux`, `bk-preview`, `bk-guard`. Row 30's performance and security examples test how requirements are worded, not performance or security work.

#### (b) Conflicts with earlier decisions

- No item-level decision existed for spec-kit (checked `docs/specs/2026-09-18-item-inventory.md` and `docs/specs/2026-09-11-skill-inventory.md`), so no row says "decided".
- The source mode ideas-only (owner, D5 question 5, 2026-09-12) is kept: zero absorb. The owner's condition for re-proposing text is new MIT plus content-fit evidence (`docs/handoff/2026-09-12.md`). In my judgment no item meets it, so no question for the owner is raised: clarify (row 31) and checklist (row 30) come closest, but their value is a list of categories and a questioning protocol the kit can state in its own words in fewer lines. Both also carry extension-hook boilerplate: 67 of clarify's 291 lines (lines 21–54 and 241–273), 66 of checklist's 379 (lines 47–80 and 348–379).
- The D3 note ("Below the line" in `skill-inventory.md`) mapped clarify to bk-spec and analyze to bk-audit; rows 31 and 29 agree. Coverage-matrix row 3 groups "constitution" with bk-spec and bk-plan; row 32 sends it to bk-setup, because the kit's counterpart to a constitution is the project's instruction files, which bk-setup owns (v2 §2, principle 3: projects hold facts).
- Tensions inside the source, to settle when porting: specify caps questions at three and asks them together, while clarify caps them at five and asks one at a time. The kit keeps one question per message (`brainstorming.md`, "Questions", rule 2), so take one cap and keep one question per message. Tasks makes tests optional unless requested, which is not carried (bk-build step 3 and bk-test put tests first). Rows 9 and 4–8 write per-item report folders under `.specify/`; that part is not carried.

#### (c) Not read

- All 38 items were read in full, plus the `assess` and `bug` extension READMEs for context and the head of `presets/README.md`.
- Outside the item definition and not read: `templates/*.md` (spec, plan, tasks, checklist and constitution templates, 591 lines), the `extensions/*.md` guides, the other extension READMEs, and the scripts. One fact from them was checked with a grep only: plan.md's gate relies on the Complexity Tracking section at `templates/plan-template.md:106`.

## addyosmani/agent-skills · đợt 2

`https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills` commit `6ca0cd7db39b`, MIT, mode **`ideas-only`** (quyết định trước: lấy ý; chỉ cân nhắc chữ khi một checklist hơn hẳn thứ kit tự viết). Bản sao: `_build/upstream/addyosmani_agent-skills` (sha khớp `upstream/sources.json`). 40 mục: 25 skill, 9 command, 4 agent, 1 hooks, 1 rule (dòng 40 thêm sau, khi script liệt kê học đếm file rule markdown).

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập (đọc cả 39 mục và các file đi kèm); phiên chính đọc lại mọi dòng idea và kiểm bằng máy rằng không dòng nào absorb. Giữ nguyên: 0 absorb, 25 idea, 14 drop; cộng dòng 40 do phiên chính thêm (drop) — 15 drop. Nguồn này trùng nhiều với những gì kit đã có hoặc vừa quyết ở mattpocock, và agent đã nêu từng chỗ trùng trong lý do.

- **Tín hiệu cho catalog:** `bk-perf` (`performance-optimization`, `web-performance-auditor` — cây triệu chứng → phép đo đầu tiên, dấu hiệu cạn pool kết nối, thiết kế cache), `bk-ops` (`ci-cd-and-automation`, `observability-and-instrumentation`, `shipping-and-launch` — một reference `bk-ops` giữ được cả ba), `bk-research` (`source-driven-development` — thứ tự thẩm quyền của nguồn, trích dẫn có anchor, nhãn UNVERIFIED), `bk-map` (`context-engineering`), `bk-product` (`idea-refine`), và nguyên liệu cho `bk-db`, `bk-deps`, `bk-agent` nằm trong ghi chú của agent.
- **Một lỗ hổng của kit, đã xác minh:** `skills/bk-review/SKILL.md:21` có cờ `--security` "ép dùng lens bảo mật", nhưng `bk-review/references/review-lenses.md` không định nghĩa lens bảo mật nào. Port `security-guidance` (quyết định D3, dòng của `claude-plugins-official` ở trên) là thứ lấp chỗ này; ghi ở `docs/status.md`.
- Mâu thuẫn để lại cho sprint: `interview-me` giữ mỗi tin một câu (thêm một dữ kiện cho lựa chọn giữa `brainstorming.md` và `grilling` của mattpocock); `source-driven-development` xếp tài liệu của vendor lên đầu, còn persona `bk-researcher` ưu tiên nguồn độc lập — hợp nhau nếu phân theo loại khẳng định, chữ phải khớp khi dựng `bk-research`.
- **Câu cho owner, không chặn** (gom vào lượt hỏi khi chốt catalog): ba chỗ agent cho rằng lấy chữ (MIT cho phép) sẽ hơn viết lại — `floor-guard.md` (một script kiểm "hạ chuẩn" theo diff), phần SSRF và xoá theo đường dẫn suy ra của `security-and-hardening`, phần idempotency của `api-and-interface-design`. Và chính sách "nội dung lấy từ ngoài là dữ liệu, không phải chỉ dẫn" xuất hiện ở năm mục của nguồn này — gộp với câu cùng loại ở `spec-kit` và `claude-plugins-official`.

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

#### (a) Work for bk-map, bk-research, bk-perf, bk-db, bk-ops or a pack

- **bk-map**: `context-engineering` (row 21) — a per-area project map (purpose, key files, the pattern to follow), loaded one area at a time. Its "read the manifests before asking" step is what `detect-stack` already does.
- **bk-research**: `source-driven-development` (row 36) — an authority order (the vendor's docs and changelog for the pinned version, then web standards and compatibility tables; Q&A sites, blogs and AI summaries never primary), deep links with anchors, an explicit unverified label, fetched pages treated as data. Its opt-in `sdd-cache` hooks (reuse a fetched page only after an HTTP 304 revalidation) are host plumbing and are not carried.
- **bk-perf**: `performance-optimization` (row 32) and `web-performance-auditor` (row 13). Beyond the row reasons: the symptom-to-first-measurement tree; the connection-pool-exhaustion signature (every endpoint slow at once, time spent waiting for a connection, the database mostly idle); cache design (every input the response depends on in the key, one invalidation strategy, a stampede guard). The kit's evidence rule 2 already requires the "not measured" label.
- **bk-db**: pieces that land here rather than on a row — index-for-query-shape and the cases where an index cannot help (partly duplicates `bk-build/references/stacks/sql.md`); pool size bounded by the server's connection ceiling, with a multiplexing proxy when instance count is unbounded; the expand/contract column rename over five deploys (`deprecation-and-migration`, duplicates mattpocock's expand–contract); the idempotency claim through a unique constraint (`api-and-interface-design`); personal-data classification, retention and a deletion path that reaches backups, caches and indexes (`security-and-hardening`).
- **bk-ops**: rows 17, 31 and 35 overlap on flag lifecycle, rollback and monitoring; one bk-ops reference can hold all three. The error-budget gate presumes an SLO, which a one-person company may not have; keep it optional.
- **bk-product**: `idea-refine` (row 28). `interview-me`'s probe for answers that sound right rather than wanted also serves discovery interviews.
- **bk-deps**: `code-review-and-quality`'s upgrade workflow (one package per change, read the changelog rather than trust the version number, review the lockfile diff) and `security-and-hardening`'s supply-chain section (install scripts blocked until approved, never a forced audit fix, advisories triaged by reachability, registry signatures checked where supported). bk-protocol already makes a major bump its own reviewed change.
- **bk-agent**: `security-and-hardening`'s LLM section and `security-auditor`'s LLM checklist — model output handled as untrusted input, the system prompt is not a security boundary, secrets and other tenants' data kept out of context, tools scoped with confirmation for destructive actions, bounded consumption, per-tenant partitions in vector stores.
- **bk-guard**: no material. The opt-in `simplify-ignore` hook beside `hooks.json` rewrites working files in place on every read and restores them on Stop, so a crash leaves placeholders on disk; not a model for the kit.

#### (b) Conflicts with earlier decisions

- No item of this source was decided before; no decision is changed.
- `interview-me` (row 30) keeps one question per message, with a guess attached. That is a data point for the open choice between `bk-spec/references/brainstorming.md` (one question per message) and mattpocock's `grilling` absorb (the whole question frontier per round, a recommended answer for each).
- `source-driven-development` ranks the vendor's documentation for the pinned version first and bars blogs and Q&A sites as primary sources; the kit's bk-researcher persona (`bk-protocol/references/personas.md:7`) prefers independent sources over a single vendor page. Compatible if read by claim type (API facts: the vendor's docs; comparative or quality claims: independent sources); the wording needs reconciling when bk-research is written.
- Not carried because they contradict kit text: `git-workflow-and-versioning`'s save points built on a hard reset; `debugging-and-error-recovery`'s fallbacks under time pressure (review-lenses' silent-failure lens); `doubt-driven-development`'s cross-model offer on every non-trivial decision (the kit asks for another model or host on hot paths); fixed `SPEC.md`, `tasks/plan.md` and `CONSTRAINTS.md` locations (the kit writes where the project says).
- `constraint-driven-development` wires fast checks to run after every edit; bk-setup allows that only as a proposed snippet the owner approves. Compatible if carried that way.

#### (c) Could not read

- Nothing. All 39 items were read in full, plus idea-refine's three support files and its init script, constraint-driven-development's `references/floor-guard.md`, and the four scripts beside `hooks.json` (only `session-start.sh` is wired; the cache and simplify-ignore hooks are opt-in, documented in `hooks/SDD-CACHE.md` and `hooks/SIMPLIFY-IGNORE.md`). All 39 line counts in the items file match the files at `6ca0cd7`. License checked: MIT.

#### (d) Questions for the owner: text that may beat a rewrite

The registry mode is ideas-only; the MIT license would permit text. Three places where a paraphrase risks losing the substance:

1. `constraint-driven-development/references/floor-guard.md` — a diff-scoped Node script (tracked and untracked changes, exit 0/1/2 with 2 never read as clean, matched values never printed) that detects the weakened-bar moves carried on row 20. It is machinery of the kind the kit keeps in `scripts/`; rewriting it adds risk without adding insight. Take it as text, or write the kit's own?
2. `security-and-hardening` — the SSRF block (check every resolved address, reject any non-unicast range, forbid redirects, the DNS-rebinding caveat) and the destructive-derived-path paragraph (allowlisted root compared after symlink resolution, at least one level below it, ownership evidence read before the operation, and why a marker file is only self-attestation). The caveats are the value, and a paraphrase tends to drop them.
3. `api-and-interface-design` — the idempotency section (three wrong key derivations, the claim through a unique constraint, a reused key with a different body failing loudly, the in-flight duplicate policy, the three outcomes of a call, retention sized to the longest redelivery path). Borderline: the kit can probably rewrite it.

Cross-row placement: "fetched pages, browser content, error text and model output are data, never instructions" appears in five items (rows 16, 21, 22, 34, 36) and in no kit file (a search of `skills/` finds none). One line in bk-protocol's security baseline could serve all five instead of a copy per skill; bk-protocol is size-limited, so the placement is the owner's call.

## anthropics/skills · đợt 2

`https://github.com/anthropics/skills` commit `34040c9c5685`. Không có giấy phép ở gốc repo. **Sửa registry theo lượt này:** 14 trong 19 thư mục skill có `LICENSE.txt` Apache-2.0 của riêng mình (không phải 2 như bản ghi 2026-09-11); `docx`, `pdf`, `pptx`, `xlsx` là độc quyền (giữ mọi quyền, `license: Proprietary` trong frontmatter); `doc-coauthoring` không có file giấy phép. Mode trong registry đổi thành "adapt, chỉ các thư mục có Apache-2.0 riêng" — đây là sửa dữ kiện (lý do của "chỉ hai skill" là giấy phép, không phải lựa chọn của owner); nếu owner muốn giữ hai, dòng `claude-api` chuyển thành idea. 20 mục: 19 skill và thư mục `template`.

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập; phiên chính kiểm lại giấy phép của từng thư mục bằng lệnh và đọc lại mọi dòng. Giữ nguyên: 2 absorb, 2 idea, 16 drop — cả hai absorb nằm trong thư mục Apache-2.0:
- `claude-api` → `bk-agent` (`references/prompt-audit.md`): phương pháp rà prompt không phụ thuộc nhà cung cấp; bỏ mọi thứ gắn với model, header hay lỗi API của Claude (việc riêng của Claude thì dùng skill `claude-api` chính chủ).
- `mcp-builder` → `bk-agent` (`references/tool-evals.md`): phương pháp đánh giá một bộ tool (câu hỏi độc lập, chỉ đọc, nhiều bước, ổn định theo thời gian). Phần thiết kế tool đã do `build-mcp-server` của `claude-plugins-official` mang, nên chỉ phần eval là mới.
- `frontend-design` giữ quyết định 2026-09-12 (trùng từng byte với bản của `claude-plugins-official`, băm lại 2026-09-18); `skill-creator` trùng từng file với bản trong `claude-plugins-official` (chỉ khác dòng copyright) — drop ở đây, bản kia mang ý.

**Bốn skill độc quyền và bản sao cục bộ.** Điều khoản của chúng cấm giữ bản sao ngoài dịch vụ của Anthropic (agent đọc thấy; phiên ghi lại, không kết luận pháp lý). Kit không cần chúng và quyết định về chúng đã xong, nên phiên đã xoá bốn thư mục khỏi cây làm việc của bản sao trong `_build/upstream/anthropics_skills` (gitignored). Kho `.git` của bản sao đó vẫn còn chứa chúng; lệnh xoá nó bị hook `scout-block` của owner chặn, phiên không lách hook — việc nhỏ cho owner, ghi ở `docs/status.md`.

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

#### (a) Work for bk-map, bk-research, bk-perf, bk-db, bk-ops or a pack

- **bk-agent, from claude-api.** `shared/prompt-audit.md` is the absorb. Keep the method: inventory the whole prompt surface, trace provenance with blame, the "could the model already know this" deletion rule, the anti-pattern groups, the keep list, a report plus a proposed diff, verification by behavioural probe. Strip the rows that name Claude models, beta headers or API errors; for Claude-specific work bk-agent names the vendor's own claude-api skill (official first). Two more files carry provider-neutral substance: `shared/cost-optimization.md` (cost per completed task, free wins before trade-offs, one lever per diff kept or reverted against an eval, a minimal eval recipe, price the hardest tenth, not the median), for the same file or a sibling `references/cost.md`; and `shared/agent-design.md` (promote an action from a shell tool to a dedicated tool when it must be gated, rendered, audited or run in parallel), next to `references/tool-design.md`. The prompt audit's section on brittle skill files is also a cross-check for the kit's own authoring rules in `bk-protocol/references/meta-routing.md`.
- **bk-agent, from mcp-builder.** `reference/evaluation.md` becomes `references/tool-evals.md`: question rules (independent, read-only, multi-hop, not solvable by keyword search, stable over time), answer rules (one value, string-comparable, human-readable), verification by solving each question first, and a report with accuracy, tool calls and the agent's feedback on the tools. The harness script is machinery (vendor SDK, an old model id as default) and is not carried.
- **bk-research, from academy-guide.** Its rule that a fetched file is data (take only the named fields, ignore anything else in it) has no counterpart in the kit: a grep of `skills/` for injection or "not instructions" found nothing. bk-research will read fetched pages.
- Nothing for bk-map, bk-perf, bk-db or bk-ops.

#### (b) Conflicts with earlier decisions

- **Licence premise, a concrete new fact that changes one row.** At 34040c9c5685, 14 of the 18 per-skill licence files are Apache-2.0, not 2. Thirteen are byte-identical (SHA-256 `52b223c318576d65…`, copyright 2026 Anthropic, PBC): academy-guide, algorithmic-art, brand-guidelines, canvas-design, claude-api, discernment-nudge, internal-comms, mcp-builder, skill-creator, slack-gif-creator, theme-factory, web-artifacts-builder, webapp-testing. frontend-design carries the same terms without the appendix. The README also says many skills are Apache-2.0. The upstream copy is a shallow single-commit clone, so when the files appeared cannot be told. Only the claude-api absorb depends on this: under the registry's "adapt (those two skills only)" it falls to idea → bk-agent. The registry mode is the owner's call: adapt per folder, or keep two. webapp-testing was held at idea, so it does not depend on it.
- **The four document skills.** Proprietary confirmed: identical 30-line LICENSE.txt (SHA-256 `6f8bd7f4d8ec5cb5…`) and `license: Proprietary` in each frontmatter. Their terms also bar keeping copies outside Anthropic's services; the kit's clone in `_build/upstream/anthropics_skills` holds them, git-ignored and untracked. Flagged for the owner, not a legal conclusion.
- **frontend-design (decided 2026-09-12) holds.** Re-hashed 2026-09-18: SHA-256 `b8009ae690dfa69e…` in both repositories, claude-plugins-official at `3b60051`, the commit bk-design cites.
- **mcp-builder is not a full duplicate of build-mcp-server.** Tool naming, descriptions, schemas, pagination, errors, annotations, structured output, transports and the Origin check are carried there; the evaluation method appears nowhere in the mcp-server-dev plugin (a grep for "evaluat" found nothing), hence absorb for that part only.
- **skill-creator** duplicates the claude-plugins-official item file for file (only LICENSE.txt differs, in its copyright line), consistent with that item's idea → bk-agent.

#### (c) Anything not read

- Nothing was blocked. Read in part, by choice: the four proprietary skills (frontmatter and licence only; excluded anyway, and their terms bar derivative works); claude-api's per-language SDK folders and its Managed Agents, model-migration, caching and tool-concept files (headings only; vendor API reference, dropped under official first); mcp-builder's node and python guides (headings only; SDK-specific, carried by build-mcp-server); binary assets (fonts, `theme-showcase.pdf`, the shadcn tarball).

---

## jeffallan/claude-skills · đợt 3

`https://github.com/jeffallan/claude-skills` commit `5e8b6b8ff400` (plugin `fullstack-dev-skills` 0.4.14), MIT, mode **`reference`**. Bản sao: `_build/upstream/jeffallan_claude-skills`, clone nông đúng sha của registry; registry trước đây chỉ ghi bản trong plugin cache của Claude Code, nay ghi thêm bản sao này. 79 mục: 66 skill, 13 command. Ba file dưới `commands/common-ground/references/` là phần của command `common-ground`, như `references/` của một skill. Mỗi file vẫn có một dòng để `check` đối được, cùng skill đích và quyết định với dòng 1.

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập (đọc cả 13 command và 66 `SKILL.md`, đọc một phần các reference sau những dòng idea). Agent này dừng giữa chừng vì hết hạn mức phiên và chạy tiếp từ ngữ cảnh cũ sau khi hạn mức reset. Phiên chính đọc lại mọi dòng. Giữ nguyên quyết định: 0 absorb, 39 idea, 40 drop. Viết lại lý do của 25 dòng drop: agent viện dẫn "stack của owner" (Kotlin với Micronaut, React và Next.js…) và "sản phẩm của owner", nhưng repo không ghi những điều đó ở đâu; agent lấy từ mẫu chỉ dẫn toàn cục của phiên. Lý do mới đứng trên những gì repo có ghi: tám stack của spec §5.5 và bảng định tuyến `skills/bk-build/references/stacks/index.md`.

- **Đổi một quyết định cũ, có dữ kiện mới.** Hàng 8 của coverage matrix (2026-09-10) định cho `code-reviewer`, `debugging-wizard` và `test-master` góp vào `bk-review`, `bk-debug`, `bk-test`. Lượt này giữ `test-master` (idea: bộ kiểm hồi quy cho auth) và drop hai skill kia. Dữ kiện: cả ba ghi obra/superpowers là nguồn phương pháp của mình (phiên chính đã `grep`: `code-reviewer` ở `SKILL.md` và hai reference, `debugging-wizard` ở `SKILL.md` và `systematic-debugging.md`), và kit đã lấy thẳng bản gốc của superpowers ngày 2026-09-11. Phần còn lại là checklist chung, và cả hai khuyên lặng lẽ điền giá trị mặc định khi thiếu, điều lens silent-failure của kit bác. Cột trạng thái của hàng 8 nay ghi kết quả này. Cụm "eight path-scoped language rules" ở hàng đó và ở `upstream/sources.json` đã cũ từ khi v2 thay lớp rule ngôn ngữ bằng stack file; sửa trong lượt này.
- **Một lỗ hổng an toàn của kit, đã xác minh.** `skills/bk-build/references/stacks/sql.md:9` bảo chạy `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` trước mà không nói `ANALYZE` thực thi câu lệnh: với `INSERT`, `UPDATE` hay `DELETE`, lệnh ghi xảy ra thật, trừ khi chạy trong một transaction rồi rollback. Ghi ở `docs/status.md` (bb); đã sửa cùng ngày (`CHANGELOG.md`, Fixed 2026-09-18).
- **Một khoảng lệch giữa v1 và v2, để owner quyết khi chốt catalog.** v1 §232 xếp Vue và Electron vào `typescript-react`; bảng định tuyến của v2 gửi một project TypeScript không có React hay Next sang `node.md`, file dành cho backend và chưa viết. Dòng 76 và 77 (Vue) giữ drop cho tới khi owner nói Vue có thuộc phạm vi hay không.
- **Tín hiệu cho catalog:** `bk-map` (`spec-miner`: test hiện có là bằng chứng của hành vi mong muốn; hành vi quan sát được viết thành yêu cầu kèm `file:line`), `bk-db` (`database-optimizer`, `postgres-pro`), `bk-perf` (`monitoring-expert`), `bk-ops` (năm dòng idea; vài mẫu của nguồn phải tránh: in secret đã giải mã, xoá mọi NetworkPolicy ở production, rollback bằng cách apply lại `HEAD~1`), `bk-product` (hai command discovery), `bk-agent` (`prompt-engineer`, `rag-architect`). Thêm đầu vào cho sáu stack file (node, python, php-laravel, c-cpp, kotlin, typescript-react) và `sql.md`. `the-fool` lấp lens "risk prediction" mà `skills/bk-audit/SKILL.md:15` gọi tên; dòng đó gọi tên sáu lens và chỉ lens reconcile có định dạng (bước 5).
- **Hai câu cho owner**, agent nêu, phiên chính giữ: (1) `the-fool` (`references/pre-mortem-analysis.md`) và bảng rủi ro bảy chiều của `create-epic-plan` là hai đoạn gần nhất với "chữ hơn hẳn viết lại". MIT cho phép lấy chữ nhưng mode là `reference`; phiên khuyến nghị giữ idea, vì cả hai ngắn và dễ viết lại. (2) Trong profile hằng ngày, plugin `fullstack-dev-skills` vẫn được cài và các skill của nó tranh cùng yêu cầu với kit: `feature-forge` và `fullstack-guardian` với `bk-spec` và `bk-build`, `code-reviewer` với `bk-review`, `debugging-wizard` với `bk-debug`, `test-master` với `bk-test`, `spec-miner` với `bk-map` sau này, `the-fool` và `architecture-designer` với `bk-audit`. Bộ activation chưa từng chạy trong profile đó (`docs/status.md` §7 (k)); nên chạy trước khi `bk-map` vào catalog.

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

#### (a) Work for bk-map, bk-research, bk-perf, bk-db, bk-ops and the packs

- **bk-map**: spec-miner (row 67) — existing tests read as evidence of intended behaviour; observed behaviour written as requirements, each with `file:line`; a coverage check (entry points, configuration, core modules read) before anything is written; an uncertainties section. It overlaps the decided code-modernization absorbs (legacy-analyst's fact versus inference, business-rules' cited rules). legacy-modernizer's assessment half (dependency map, integrations and data contracts documented before planning) duplicates modernize-preflight and modernize-assess; its characterization tests went to bk-test (row 44). create-epic-plan's codebase-analysis section (affected modules, patterns, reference implementations, test locations) is a feature-scoped map, the same idea as feature-dev's code-explorer (batch 1).
- **bk-research**: no item targets it. the-fool's evidence audit (claims, falsification criteria, evidence grades, competing explanations) and common-ground's origin tags (stated, inferred, assumed, uncertain) fit the bk-researcher persona's confidence labels if the skill is built.
- **bk-perf**: monitoring-expert (row 48) — load, stress, spike or soak runs chosen by the question asked; thresholds inside the script that fail the run; arrival-rate load for APIs, so a slow server does not lower the offered load; profilers per runtime; performance budgets. Not to take: its capacity arithmetic sizes database connections without the server's ceiling (addyosmani's pool note stands), and its Prometheus sample labels metrics with the raw request path, which is unbounded. database-optimizer (row 25, targeted at bk-db) is shared material.
- **bk-db**: database-optimizer (row 25) and postgres-pro (row 54) — pg_stat_statements as the starting point; one change per measurement, reverted when writes slow or replication lags; index use confirmed from `idx_scan`, which also finds unused indexes; dead tuples and autovacuum; transaction-ID wraparound age; blocking locks; replication lag; restore tests on a schedule; the pooling mode. Rewrite, do not copy: its blocking-locks query predates `pg_blocking_pids()`. sql-pro (row 69) goes to `sql.md`. **Kit gap noticed in passing:** `skills/bk-build/references/stacks/sql.md:9` puts `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` first without saying that ANALYZE executes the statement — on an INSERT, UPDATE or DELETE it performs the write unless run inside a transaction that is rolled back. None of the three database items says so either.
- **bk-ops**: five ideas — cloud-architect (20), devops-engineer (27), kubernetes-specialist (42), sre-engineer (70), terraform-engineer (72); chaos-engineer dropped. They overlap addyosmani rows 17, 31 and 35 (rollback before deploy, observability, flags). New here: the saved Terraform plan applied exactly as read; state surgery (`state rm`, `import`, force-unlock) treated as COUNCIL; pinned providers and locked, encrypted state; a Kubernetes triage order and RBAC checked with `auth can-i`; burn-rate alerts over two windows, each with a runbook; a disaster-recovery test that records the actual recovery time; cost by allocation tag; immutable image tags; a post-deploy smoke test. Not to take, all in this source: the Kubernetes troubleshooting reference decodes a production secret to the terminal and deletes every NetworkPolicy in production "temporarily"; devops-engineer's evidence script captures packets in production pods and dumps `pg_stat_activity` query text to files, its CI sample pins a third-party action to `@master`, and its Terraform rollback re-applies `HEAD~1`; sre-engineer's remediation script restarts a deployment on every error spike. terraform-engineer locks S3 state through DynamoDB; newer Terraform may lock natively in S3 (unverified here) — a version-card check for whoever writes bk-ops.
- **bk-product**: create-epic-discovery (row 6, the hypothesis map) and synthesize-discovery (row 7, findings traced to hypotheses, conflicts surfaced); approve-synthesis is the Jira tail and is dropped.
- **bk-agent**: prompt-engineer (row 55) and rag-architect (row 57). fine-tuning-expert, ml-pipeline and mcp-developer are dropped (model training and MLOps lie beyond the pack; MCP servers are covered by the decided build-mcp-server absorb).
- **Stack files (bk-build)**, the reading inputs the content program plans: node.md (javascript-pro, cli-developer), python.md (python-pro), php-laravel.md (php-pro, laravel-specialist), c-cpp.md (cpp-pro), kotlin.md (kotlin-specialist), typescript-react.md (typescript-pro, react-expert, nextjs-developer), sql.md (sql-pro). shell.md has no expert here beyond cli-developer's output rules. Each of these items carries at least one sample the kit should not copy (named in the row reasons and above), so the stack files stay the kit's own text.
- **bk-preview**: common-ground's reasoning graph (row 4) is the only visual artifact in the source; its edge weights are invented numbers (evidence rule 2).
- Nothing for bk-ux, bk-deps (security-reviewer's scanner list is a tool catalog the kit does not keep) or bk-guard.

#### (b) Conflicts with earlier decisions

- Coverage matrix row 8 (2026-09-10, status "designed") says the code-reviewer, debugging-wizard and test-master lenses inform bk-review, bk-debug and bk-test. This inventory keeps test-master (row 73, idea) and drops the other two (rows 22, 26). The new fact: all three credit obra/superpowers for their method references (comments in their reference tables), and the kit absorbed those superpowers originals on 2026-09-11 (`code-review-exchange.md`, `executing.md`, `systematic-debugging.md`, `tdd.md`); debugging-wizard's bisect harness is also in mattpocock's diagnosing-bugs absorb (decided 2026-09-18). What remains of the two dropped items is generic, and both recommend defaulting a missing value silently, which the silent-failure lens rejects; test-master keeps a row only for its security test set. The same matrix row and `upstream/sources.json` still say the kit carries "eight path-scoped language rules"; v2 replaced that layer with the bk-build stack files (spec §4, §5.5), so the wording is stale.
- Rows 2–4 share row 1's target and decision on the coordinator's instruction. Judged alone, file-management (row 3) would be a drop, since it writes into the user's home profile, and so would reasoning-graph (row 4), for its invented weights; the idea carried comes from COMMAND.md and assumption-classification.
- legacy-modernizer's characterization tests (row 44) take expected values from the current code, bugs included, while mattpocock's decided tdd absorb takes expected values from an independent source. Compatible if characterization tests are labelled as pinning current behaviour rather than asserting correctness; the wording needs care when bk-test is edited.
- api-designer (row 15) extends addyosmani row 15 (idea, decided 2026-09-18) without changing it; its REST rules (resource URIs, path parameters) yield to a project's own API conventions, as `sql.md` already says for schemas.
- secure-code-guardian (row 63) adds to the security lens decided 2026-09-11 (D3) from security-guidance; security-reviewer (row 64) is dropped against that same decision.

#### (c) Could not read

- Nothing among the 79 items: all 13 command files and all 66 `SKILL.md` files were read; the long code samples of a few dropped experts (salesforce, spark, shopify, rails, react-native, vue-expert-js, wordpress, websocket) were only skimmed. Every skill's `references/` list was checked (366 files, counted, matching `version.json`); the references behind the idea rows and the three matrix-named lenses were read in part. All 79 line counts in the items file match the files at `5e8b6b8ff400`; license MIT (`LICENSE` at the root).
- Not items and not read: `.claude/old-commands/legacy/` (five older copies of the project commands; the script's `commands` segment does not match `old-commands`), `commands/intake/*.yaml` and the `.yaml` manifest beside each command, and the repository's `site/`, `docs/`, `research/` and `specs/` folders.

#### (d) Questions for the owner

1. **Text that might beat a rewrite.** The mode is reference, so nothing is vendored. No item's text clearly beats what the kit can write: most samples carry defects (row reasons and (a) name them). The two closest are short and paraphrasable — the-fool's `references/pre-mortem-analysis.md` (its specificity checklist and the worked migration-data-loss narrative) and create-epic-plan's seven-dimension risk table with its three levels. Keep them as ideas, or take either as text under MIT?
2. **Routing while the plugin stays installed.** Its descriptions compete with kit intents in the daily profile: feature-forge and fullstack-guardian (whose triggers include "implement feature" and "new feature") with bk-spec and bk-build; code-reviewer with bk-review; debugging-wizard with bk-debug; test-master with bk-test; spec-miner ("understand codebase") with a future bk-map; the-fool and architecture-designer with bk-audit. The only daily-profile reading (`docs/status.md` §7 (k)) saw bk-spec win one React prompt; the activation set has not run there. Worth a run before bk-map enters the catalog.

## vercel-labs/agent-skills · đợt 3

`https://github.com/vercel-labs/agent-skills` commit `063bee94c3f4`, mode **`reference`**. Registry ghi "không có file giấy phép", và điều đó vẫn đúng; **dữ kiện mới, phiên chính đã kiểm bằng lệnh:** `README.md` (mục License), `skills/vercel-optimize/README.md`, frontmatter của bốn skill (`composition-patterns`, `react-best-practices`, `react-native-skills`, `react-view-transitions`) và hai `packages/*/package.json` đều khai "MIT", nhưng cả cây không có văn bản giấy phép hay dòng copyright nào. MIT chỉ có một điều kiện là giữ thông báo bản quyền, mà thông báo đó không tồn tại, nên mọi dòng giữ ở idea. Một khai báo "MIT" trần có đủ làm giấy phép hay không là câu của owner (nếu đủ, dòng 1 và 3 thành ứng viên absorb); registry ghi thêm dữ kiện này. Bản sao: `_build/upstream/vercel-labs_agent-skills` (sha khớp). 9 mục: 9 skill.

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập; phiên chính đọc lại mọi dòng và sửa hai dòng:
- dòng 8 (`web-design-guidelines`) từ drop thành **idea → `bk-design`**: hàng 9 của coverage matrix (2026-09-10) ghi `bk-design` trích nó như tài liệu đọc, và D3 chốt "trích như một pointer, không bao giờ chép cách fetch" (`docs/specs/2026-09-11-skill-inventory.md`, bảng Collisions). Một pointer là idea. Pointer đó chưa có trong `bk-design` (`grep` trên `skills/` không thấy), ghi ở `docs/status.md`.
- dòng 4 (`react-native-skills`): lý do của agent viện dẫn một "kế hoạch Swift và Flutter" không có trong spec; viết lại theo §5.5. Quyết định giữ drop.

Kết quả: 0 absorb, 4 idea, 5 drop.

- **Bản ghi cũ đã lỗi thời, sửa trong lượt này:** trường `read` của nguồn này trong `upstream/sources.json` và cột trạng thái hàng 9 của coverage matrix vẫn ghi "chưa đặt vào đâu, `stacks/` chưa tồn tại". Thật ra `typescript-react.md` trích `react-best-practices` từ 2026-09-15 (`735f99c`, dòng 5 của file).
- **Tín hiệu cho catalog:** `bk-perf` (`vercel-optimize`: tín hiệu production trước khi đọc code, ngưỡng cố định chọn mục tiêu, mục bị giữ lại vẫn có tên và lý do), `bk-agent` (kiểm output của model bằng máy, không để model tự chấm), `bk-ops` (preview là mặc định, token đi qua môi trường chứ không qua argv, tìm nguyên nhân của một đợt tăng tải trước khi sửa), `bk-db` (so p95 CPU với p95 độ trễ trước khi đổ lỗi cho database).
- **Bẫy cho version card của `typescript-react.md`:** App Router của Next.js chạy một bản React canary đóng gói bên trong Next, nên major `react` trong lockfile không mô tả thứ App Router chạy. Kiểm tài liệu Next.js trước khi ghi version card.

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

#### (a) Work for bk-map, bk-research, bk-perf, bk-db, bk-ops or a pack

- **bk-perf, from vercel-optimize (row 7).** Its `references/doctrine.md` is the method, and it is provider-neutral once the Vercel parts are removed: no source file is read until a production signal points at it (grepping for known anti-patterns gives noisy, low-impact advice); fixed thresholds, not the model's judgement, choose what gets investigated, and every held-back candidate appears in the report with its reason; an investigation reads only the file the signal names and its imports, and wanting to search the whole repository means the candidate is malformed; each recommendation carries the observed number, `file:line` and a document that applies to the pinned framework version; improvements are ranges from the observed baseline, costs are magnitudes, never invented dollar precision. Two caveats from the same skill: caching is a safety question before a speed one (public, cacheable GET only; rule out cookies, sessions, authorization, draft state and per-viewer output; name the mechanism, scope, lifetime and `Vary`; no-store is a valid answer), and a long-lived streaming route is not slow on wall-clock time alone. Not carried: `lib/` and `scripts/` (90 code files, 15,079 lines by `wc -l`), the Vercel metrics CLI and its paid observability tier, sub-agent fan-out, and the customer-copy rules that keep terms such as "LLM" and "sub-agent" out of the report.
- **bk-perf, from react-best-practices (row 3).** The category order generalises beyond React: the request chain first, then what ships, then server work, then client fetching, then re-renders. The stack file already applies it to React; if bk-perf is written, it can own the order across stacks while the stack files keep the rules.
- **bk-db, from vercel-optimize's `database-egress-pooling-region` topic.** Before blaming the database, compare CPU p95 with latency p95 to separate downstream I/O from in-process work; propose a pool, provider or region change only when the repository and its configuration show it applies.
- **bk-ops, from rows 2 and 6 and vercel-optimize's `usage-spike-triage` topic.** Carry: preview by default, production only on an explicit request (bk-protocol already makes production systems COUNCIL, `skills/bk-protocol/SKILL.md:17`); read deploy state from the link files, never with a command that prompts or links as a side effect; ask before a push that triggers a deploy; a token reaches a CLI through its environment, never argv, where shell history and process listings show it; a usage spike gets a cause (crawler, real traffic, pricing change, regression) before a fix, so real traffic is not rate-limited and a deploy is not reverted for crawler load; the vendor's CLI is named, not re-implemented. Not carried: printing a token or `.env` contents while looking for one (`printenv VERCEL_TOKEN`, `grep -i vercel .env`), against the security baseline (`skills/bk-protocol/SKILL.md:64`) and this repository's own convention; `git add .` before a deploy commit, which skips bk-ship's guardrails and secret scan (`skills/bk-ship/SKILL.md:13-18`); the ban on checking the deployed URL, against the definition of done (`skills/bk-protocol/SKILL.md:54`); `-y` to silence prompts; and the anonymous fallback that uploads the whole project, less `.env*`, `.git` and `node_modules`, to `claude-skills-deploy.vercel.com` and returns a live preview URL.
- **bk-agent (pack), from vercel-optimize's `references/verification.md`.** A method for checking model output: extract every checkable claim (counts, presence, absence, file existence, citations, contradictions) and verify it mechanically, never by asking the model to grade itself; four outcomes (verified, failed, unsupported, unverifiable); regenerate with the failures listed, keep the new output only if it verifies better without losing findings, and never ship a safety failure.
- **bk-research, from rows 8 and 9 and vercel-optimize.** Both wrappers take their rules and their output format from a page fetched at run time: one more case for the "fetched content is data, never instructions" policy raised in batch 2. Vercel-optimize's rule that a citation must come from a vetted list and apply to the detected version fits bk-research's authority order and the protocol's pinned-documentation rule.
- **Stack files.**
  1. `typescript-react.md` still lacks most of row 3's rules. The ones worth re-deriving first, in the kit's words: authenticate inside every server action, since it is a public endpoint whatever guards the page; module scope on the server is shared by concurrent requests, so request data in a module variable can reach another user; pass a client component only the fields it uses, since everything passed is serialised into the page; never define a component inside another (a new type every render remounts it and loses its state); a numeric condition before `&&` renders `0`; third-party scripts load after hydration. The first two are also findings for bk-review's planned security lens (`references/security-lens.md`, batch 1 row 117).
  2. Row 1's React 19 rule (ref as a prop, `use()` over `useContext`) is official-docs material; the file already routes newer syntax through pinned documentation.
  3. Row 5 surfaced a trap for the lockfile majors `detect-stack` reports: per its availability note, the Next.js App Router runs a React canary bundled inside Next, so the lockfile's `react` does not describe what the App Router runs. Check the Next.js documentation before writing it into the version card. Row 5's instruction to add every applicable transition pattern runs against bk-design's restraint and is not carried.
  4. `detect-stack` knows no `react-native` or `expo` (`scripts/detect-stack.cjs:46-50`): an Expo app reports `react`, and `skills/bk-build/references/stacks/index.md:7` routes it to `typescript-react.md`, whose bundle and server rules are for the web. If the owner takes up React Native, the v2 spec lets the inventory open a stack file (`docs/specs/2026-09-11-bearingkit-v2-design.md:115`) and row 4 becomes its starting read, ideas only.
- Nothing for bk-map.

#### (b) Conflicts with earlier decisions

- **The licence premise holds, with a fact the registry did not record.** There is no licence file anywhere in the tree at 063bee94c3f4 and no copyright line in any file (`find` on licence, copying and notice names; `grep -i copyright`); the clone is shallow, one commit, so history cannot be checked. Yet the root `README.md` License section says MIT, as does `skills/vercel-optimize/README.md`; four SKILL.md frontmatters carry `license: MIT` (composition-patterns, react-best-practices, react-native-skills, react-view-transitions); and two `packages/*/package.json` files say MIT. The 2026-09-11 checks looked only for a root `LICENSE` file and the API's licence field. MIT's one condition is to keep a copyright and permission notice that does not exist here, so every row stays at idea, as the task requires. Whether a bare "MIT" declaration is a usable grant is the owner's call; if it were, rows 1 and 3 would be the absorb candidates. A licence file upstream would settle it.
- **react-best-practices, decided 2026-09-11 (D3 row 6, `docs/specs/2026-09-11-skill-inventory.md:106`), holds; its landing is partial.** `typescript-react.md` (commit `735f99c`, 2026-09-15) cites it and carries the category order and a handful of rules in its own words; the rule-by-rule clean-room pass D3 asked for is not done (stack-file bullet 1 above). Two records have been stale since 2026-09-15: the `read` field of this source in `upstream/sources.json` ("not yet placed … doesn't exist yet") and the status column of coverage-matrix row 9 (`docs/specs/2026-09-10-coverage-matrix.md:21`).
- **web-design-guidelines: decided, not applied.** Coverage-matrix row 9 (2026-09-10) says bk-design cites it as reading, and D3 (`docs/specs/2026-09-11-skill-inventory.md:121`) set the pointer form; a grep of `skills/` finds no mention of it or of the Web Interface Guidelines. Row 8 keeps the decision; the pointer is still to be written.
- **The owner named this source mandatory** (#19, `docs/handoff/2026-09-11-owner-directives.md:14`). The mandate is at source level and D3 already graded the source ideas-only; this batch carries it through three idea rows (1, 3, 7) and drops six on the reasons given, surfaced here so the owner sees them.

#### (c) Anything not read

- The rules behind rows 8 and 9 live in `vercel-labs/web-interface-guidelines` and `vercel-labs/writing-guidelines`: not in this copy, not in `upstream/sources.json`, not fetched (the brief confines reading to the source copy); their licences are unknown.
- `skills/deploy-to-vercel/Archive.zip` is binary: listed, not extracted. It holds an older packed copy of the same SKILL.md and both scripts, plus macOS metadata.
- Read in part, by choice: vercel-optimize's SKILL.md and `doctrine.md` in full, parts of `verification.md`, three support topics, its other references by headings, its code not at all; the deploy scripts for their endpoint and exclude list only; the three rule-based skills (rows 1, 3, 4) through SKILL.md in full plus the prose of a sample of rule files, not their compiled `AGENTS.md`; row 5 through its SKILL.md prose, its five references by name only.
- Two self-counts disagree in the source: react-native-skills indexes 33 rules but ships 36 rule files; react-best-practices' `metadata.json` says 40+ rules against 70 in SKILL.md and on disk (the second already noted by the 2026-09-11 audit).

## cloudflare/skills · đợt 3

`https://github.com/cloudflare/skills` commit `b052c32bab7d`, Apache-2.0 (`LICENSE` ở gốc repo và `plugin.json`; không thư mục skill nào có giấy phép riêng), mode **`reference`**: chỉ lấy ý, do registry chọn, dù Apache-2.0 cho phép lấy chữ. Bản sao: `_build/upstream/cloudflare_skills` (sha khớp `upstream/sources.json`). 15 mục: 14 skill, 1 rule.

**Duyệt của phiên chính.** Bảng do một agent nghiên cứu lập (đọc cả 15 mục và mọi reference của `agents-sdk`, `sandbox-next`, `workers-best-practices`; 57 thư mục sản phẩm của skill `cloudflare` chỉ đọc mục lục, riêng phần observability đọc hết); phiên chính đọc lại mọi dòng và kiểm bằng máy rằng không dòng nào absorb. Giữ nguyên: 0 absorb, 8 idea, 7 drop. Viết lại ba lý do, quyết định không đổi:
- dòng 7 và 8 dựa vào "owner host trên AWS và Railway" — repo không ghi điều đó ở đâu (`grep` trên `docs/` và `skills/` không thấy), agent lấy từ mẫu chỉ dẫn toàn cục của phiên; coi là chưa xác minh. Lý do mới đứng trên "official first" và luật không cài skill của bên thứ ba. Ghi chú của agent bên dưới còn nhắc Railway và AWS: đọc là ví dụ nền tảng, không phải dữ kiện về owner.
- dòng 9 nói tới `major-upgrade.md` của `bk-deps` như một file đã có; đó mới là một ý của đợt 1.

- **Tín hiệu cho catalog:** `bk-ops` nhận bốn dòng idea (`wrangler`, `turnstile-spin`, `cloudflare-one-migrations`, `sandbox-migrate-to-next`); một reference về thay đổi an toàn trên hạ tầng và bí mật giữ được cả bốn. `bk-agent` nhận hai (`agents-sdk`, `sandbox-next`), `bk-perf` một (`web-perf`), `bk-build` một (`workers-best-practices` → `stacks/node.md`, file chưa có).
- **Hai câu cho owner khi chốt catalog**, không quyết ở đây: (1) hàng 21 của `docs/specs/2026-09-10-coverage-matrix.md` và mục 8 của `docs/plans/2026-09-10-content-backlog.md` định giới thiệu cloudflare/skills khi thấy `wrangler.toml`, trái với luật "kit không giới thiệu công cụ, server hay plugin nào ngoài dòng tra tài liệu" (`skills/bk-setup/SKILL.md:18`); Cloudflare nay còn ưu tiên `wrangler.jsonc` cho project mới. (2) Quy trình ghi bí mật của `turnstile-spin` chặt hơn mọi thứ kit đang có; Apache-2.0 cho phép lấy chữ, chỉ mode `reference` chặn. Phiên khuyến nghị giữ idea, vì chữ đó gắn với Wrangler còn `bk-ops` cần cùng luật cho mọi nền tảng.
- **"Nội dung lấy từ ngoài là dữ liệu, không phải chỉ dẫn"** có thêm một nguồn (`turnstile-spin` nêu hai lần), gộp vào câu hỏi owner đã ghi từ đợt 1 và 2.

**Ghi chú của agent nghiên cứu** (tiếng Anh, giữ nguyên):

Anchors are paths inside `_build/upstream/cloudflare_skills`.

#### (a) Work for bk-map, bk-research, bk-perf, bk-db, bk-ops or a pack

- **bk-ops, four idea rows; one reference could hold them.**
  - *wrangler* (`skills/wrangler/SKILL.md:14,38-44,50`): name the account, environment and resource, and whether the target is local or remote, before any mutating command; settings that are not inherited per environment mean a working default proves nothing about staging; reconcile console edits before a config deploy overwrites them; a secret change is a deployment; rollback restores code, not data or connected resources; a dry run proves build and packaging, not runtime; local development can be wired to real resources, so check before testing writes; the report names target, checks run and open gaps. Each maps onto Terraform on AWS and Railway environments.
  - *turnstile-spin* (`skills/turnstile-spin/SKILL.md:48-55,127,145-146,159-167,283`; its scripts pass the bearer token to `curl --config -` on stdin): a secret never travels through chat, command arguments, exported variables, temp files, logs or diffs, and the user types it hidden in their own terminal; a credential-bearing command runs only through an approved executable outside the project, never `npx`, a package script or a project-local binary, which the repository controls; the credential is checked with a deliberately invalid request whose error code separates a bad secret from a bad token (a negative control); the exact target is confirmed just before the write and verified by name after; `git check-ignore` gates any write to an env file; a write manifest gets a fresh confirmation, never one inferred from an earlier step. Complements mattpocock `wizard` (idea → bk-ops, 2026-09-18); the ignore check meets the bk-setup candidate from spec-kit row 34.
  - *cloudflare-one-migrations* (`skills/cloudflare-one-migrations/SKILL.md:13-18,84,88,93`), for moves such as Railway to AWS or a DNS or CDN change: structured exports over screenshots; every source object maps to a target or to an explicit not-migrated row with reason and security impact; counts compared at each stage, stop on a mismatch; new rules created disabled or audit-only under a prefix, piloted, logs compared, rolled back by prefix; no allow-all catch-all to keep traffic flowing. The dropped *cloudflare-one* adds two habits for the same reference: a new blocking policy runs log-only and is tuned before it blocks (`skills/cloudflare-one/SKILL.md:76,125`; AWS WAF's Count action and CSP report-only are the same move), and a denial is traced from its log entry back to the responsible rule (`:151`).
  - *sandbox-migrate-to-next* (`skills/sandbox-migrate-to-next/SKILL.md:31`): a staged rollout presumes old and new versions coexist; when they cannot in either direction, make them compatible first (expand/contract) or cut over at once, with the owner's consent that in-flight work may stop. addyosmani's staged-rollout idea (row 35) lacks this precondition.
  - From the dropped *cloudflare* umbrella (`skills/cloudflare/references/observability/gotchas.md:25`): sampling cuts coverage as well as volume, so the absence of a sampled event is not proof that no error happened. Belongs with addyosmani's observability idea (row 31).
- **bk-agent (pack), two idea rows.**
  - *agents-sdk*: checkpoint durable work and re-derive the next step from the checkpoint, because the running closure is not restored (`references/durable-execution.md:47-49`); external side effects inside durable steps, retried operations idempotent, progress reports expected to repeat (`references/workflows.md:19-21`); retry budgets priced in cost and latency, failures recorded explicitly where no dead-letter queue exists (`references/queue-retries.md:13-16`); an approval that times out is resolved before the gated action (`references/human-in-the-loop.md:10`); cancellation forwarded to the model call, and client reconnect kept separate from host eviction (`references/streaming-chat.md:16`); server-initiated turns wait for the conversation to settle (`references/server-driven-messages.md:7`). Code mode (`references/codemode.md`): model-written code over tools only for chained or conditional use, run with outbound network blocked; its own limitation at line 108 (approval-gated tools run immediately inside generated code) is the lesson that an approval gate must hold on every execution path. Inbound channels (`references/email.md`, `references/webhooks-push.md`): verify a signature, deduplicate by event id, acknowledge fast and process later, skip auto-replies to avoid loops, route replies through signed headers with an expiry. Caution when rewriting: the webhook sample reads the request body twice (`references/webhooks-push.md:29,32`); a body can be read once, so verify and parse the same bytes. The email service's "store a draft, a person sends it" flow (`skills/cloudflare-email-service/references/routing.md:109-192`) is the same approval rule.
  - *sandbox-next* (`skills/sandbox-next/SKILL.md:40,42,43,45`): live credentials stay in the host, and sandboxed code reaches external APIs through a host-side proxy that attaches them; a timeout on waiting does not stop the process, so kill it explicitly; work that must survive a replaced container is persisted as the full job spec, not a process id; errors are classified before any retry.
- **bk-perf, web-perf (idea).** Complements addyosmani's web-performance-auditor (idea → bk-perf, 2026-09-18): cold-load trace with reload; LCP split into server response, resource load and render delay; zero-impact findings noted, not recommended; an origin is called unused only when zero requests reached it (`skills/web-perf/SKILL.md:126`); missing trace tools are stated, not silently worked around (`:20`); each recommendation names the file and its size. Its threshold table (`:103-110`) copies numbers that change; bk-perf should cite the metric's owner instead.
- **bk-research.** wrangler and workers-best-practices sharpen evidence rule 5: documentation describes the latest release, while the installed binary's help, config schema and generated types show what the project can use; a newer type package does not override the configured target (`skills/wrangler/SKILL.md:33`, `skills/workers-best-practices/SKILL.md:20`).
- **bk-deps (pack).** sandbox-migrate-to-next's lockstep rule (a client package and the runtime image it talks to move on the same version line) and its grep for removed APIs after the port (`SKILL.md:30,170`), for `major-upgrade.md`.
- **Existing skills, from rows above.** bk-build `stacks/node.md` (row 14, `references/runtime-patterns.md:188-286`): module-level mutable request state leaks between requests on any reused process; a promise neither awaited, returned nor tied to a lifetime drops work and swallows errors, worst on hosts that freeze or recycle the process after the response; stream unbounded bodies; CSPRNG for tokens; constant-time comparison after hashing both sides to a fixed length. The last two, plus turnstile-spin's app side (server-side verification only, fail closed on verifier errors, token bound to the expected action and hostname, no localhost in a production allowlist, a replayed token rejected in the end-to-end check, `SKILL.md:84-129`), belong in the planned `bk-review/references/security-lens.md`. For bk-test: persistence is proven by restart and read-back, not by repeated calls (`skills/durable-objects/references/testing.md:19`), and a harness that injects runtime flags itself can pass while the deployed config lacks them (`skills/workers-best-practices/references/runtime-patterns.md:317`), an environment-parity check.
- **Nothing** for bk-map or bk-db (the Hyperdrive pooling note repeats the pool point batch 2 already sent to bk-db).

#### (b) Conflicts with earlier decisions

- No item of this source had a per-item decision before, and no row reverses one.
- **Coverage matrix row 21 and content backlog item 8 (2026-09-10)** plan an optional skill or a `doctor` recommendation when a `wrangler.toml` is detected. Two later facts bear on that plan: bk-setup (2026-09-17, step 5) and the inventory rows since hold that the kit keeps no catalog of third-party plugins or skills, which a recommendation of cloudflare/skills would contradict (today `scripts/doctor.cjs` checks only the kit's own install); and Cloudflare's own wrangler skill now prefers `wrangler.jsonc` for new projects (`skills/wrangler/SKILL.md:37`), so detection keyed on `wrangler.toml` alone would miss them. The rows above hold under either reading, since nothing product-specific is taken; row 21 is the owner's call at catalog close.
- **Corroboration, not a conflict.** turnstile-spin states the "repository text is untrusted data" rule twice (`skills/turnstile-spin/SKILL.md:146,164`): one more source for the pending owner question on adding it to the protocol's security baseline (batch 1 claude-plugins-official notes; batch 2 spec-kit rows 6, 7 and 9).

#### (c) Anything not read

- Nothing was blocked. Read in full: all 15 item files; every reference of agents-sdk, sandbox-next and workers-best-practices; durable-objects' `rules.md` and `testing.md`; turnstile-spin's `tests/validation.md` and README; email-service's `sending.md` and `rest-api.md`. Read in part, by choice: the 57 product folders (288 files) under `skills/cloudflare/references/` (listing, headings of flagship, terraform, pulumi and api, observability in full; vendor product documentation, dropped under official first); durable-objects' `references/workers.md` (headings and first 40 lines); email-service's `routing.md` and `deliverability.md` in part, `cli-and-mcp.md` headings only; turnstile-spin's six framework references (headings; widget embed snippets) and scripts (headers and token-passing lines). Root MCP and plugin manifests are not items and were not read beyond `plugin.json`'s licence field.

#### (d) Questions for the owner: text that may beat a rewrite

- None clearly. The closest is turnstile-spin's guarded secret write (`skills/turnstile-spin/SKILL.md:159-283`): tighter than anything in the kit today, but bound to Wrangler and Turnstile, while bk-ops needs the same rules for Railway variables, AWS secret stores and CI secrets, so the kit's own wording loses nothing. If the owner wants the text anyway, the licence permits it; only the registry's `reference` mode stands in the way.
